CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE rebuild_indexes(p_tablename IN VARCHAR2) AS
	tablename VARCHAR2(100);
BEGIN
    tablename := upper(p_tablename);
    --索引
    FOR index_rec IN (SELECT INDEX_NAME FROM USER_INDEXES WHERE TABLE_NAME = tablename AND PARTITIONED = 'N';)
    LOOP
		BEGIN
			DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('alter index ' || index_rec.index_name || ' rebuild;');
			EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'alter index ' || index_rec.index_name || ' rebuild';
			EXCEPTION
				WHEN OTHERS THEN
				    --DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('SQLCODE' || SQLCODE);
					IF SQLCODE = 2012 OR SQLCODE = 2201 THEN -- 索引不存在 或者 需要按子分区重建
					   CONTINUE; -- 忽略该错误并继续执行下一个分区索引的重建
					ELSE
					   RAISE; -- 抛出其他异常
					END IF;
		END;
    END LOOP;
    --分区索引
    FOR index_rec IN (SELECT INDEX_NAME, PARTITION_NAME FROM USER_IND_PARTITIONS WHERE INDEX_NAME IN (SELECT INDEX_NAME FROM USER_INDEXES WHERE TABLE_NAME = TABLENAME))
    LOOP
		BEGIN
			DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('alter index ' || index_rec.index_name || ' rebuild partition ' || index_rec.partition_name || ';');
			EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'alter index ' || index_rec.index_name || ' rebuild partition ' || index_rec.partition_name;
			EXCEPTION
				WHEN OTHERS THEN
				    --DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('SQLCODE' || SQLCODE);
					IF SQLCODE = 2012 OR SQLCODE = 2201 THEN -- 索引不存在 或者 需要按子分区重建
					   CONTINUE; -- 忽略该错误并继续执行下一个分区索引的重建
					ELSE
					   RAISE; -- 抛出其他异常
					END IF;
		END;
    END LOOP;
	--子分区索引
    FOR index_rec IN (SELECT INDEX_NAME, SUBPARTITION_NAME FROM USER_IND_SUBPARTITIONS WHERE INDEX_NAME IN (SELECT INDEX_NAME FROM USER_INDEXES WHERE TABLE_NAME = TABLENAME))
    LOOP
		BEGIN
			DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('alter index ' || index_rec.index_name || ' rebuild subpartition ' || index_rec.subpartition_name || ';');
			EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'alter index ' || index_rec.index_name || ' rebuild subpartition ' || index_rec.subpartition_name;
			EXCEPTION
				WHEN OTHERS THEN
				    --DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('SQLCODE' || SQLCODE);
					IF SQLCODE = 2012 OR SQLCODE = 2201 THEN -- 索引不存在 或者 需要按子分区重建
					   CONTINUE; -- 忽略该错误并继续执行下一个分区索引的重建
					ELSE
					   RAISE; -- 抛出其他异常
					END IF;
		END;
    END LOOP;
END rebuild_indexes;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE get_partition_tbs_count(p_tablename IN VARCHAR2) IS
    partname VARCHAR2(100);
    tbsname VARCHAR2(100);
    tablename VARCHAR2(100);
    highvalue VARCHAR2(100);
    num_rows NUMBER;
BEGIN
    tablename := upper(p_tablename);
  --分区
    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('PARTITION---------------------------------------------------------------');
    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('PARTITION_NAME    TABLESPACE_NAME    NUM_ROWS    HIGH_VALUE');
    FOR partition_rec IN (SELECT PARTITION_NAME, TABLESPACE_NAME, HIGH_VALUE FROM USER_TAB_PARTITIONS WHERE TABLE_NAME = tablename AND PARTITION_NAME not like 'SYS_%';)
    LOOP
        partname := partition_rec.PARTITION_NAME;
        tbsname := partition_rec.TABLESPACE_NAME;
        highvalue := partition_rec.HIGH_VALUE;
        EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ' || tablename || ' PARTITION (' || partname || ')' INTO num_rows;
        DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE(partname || '          ' || tbsname ||  '          ' || num_rows ||  '          ' || highvalue);
    END LOOP;

END get_partition_tbs_count;
/

--@testPoint 高级包
--@testPoint 包头
create or replace package var_pkg
is
function var_test1(c11 int) return int;
function var_test2(c22 bigint) return bigint;
function var_test3(c33 double) return double;
function var_test4(c44 float) return float;
end;
/

--@testPoint 包体
create or replace package body var_pkg
is

function var_test1(c11 int) return int as
a11 int;
begin
select variance(c11) into a11
from dual;
dbms_output.put_line('variance='||a11);
return a11;
end;


function var_test2(c22 bigint) return bigint as
a22 bigint;
begin
select variance(c22) into a22
from dual;
dbms_output.put_line('variance='||a22);
return a22;
end;

function var_test3(c33 double) return double as
a33 double;
begin
select variance(c33) into a33
from dual;
dbms_output.put_line('variance='||a33);
return a33;
end;

function var_test4(c44 float) return float as
a44 bigint;
begin
select variance(c44) into a44
from dual;
dbms_output.put_line('variance='||a44);
return a44;
end;

end;
/


--@testPoint 调用包
declare
   b11 int;
   b22 bigint;
   b33 double;
   b44 float;
  

begin
     select c1,id,c4,c3 into b11,b22,b33,b44 from test_var_pkg_t1;
     b11:=var_pkg.var_test1(5);
     b22:=var_pkg.var_test2(5);
     b33:=var_pkg.var_test3(5);
     b44:=var_pkg.var_test4(5.1);
     end;
     /

   
--@testPoint 清除表
drop table test_var_pkg_t1;
drop package var_pkg;

--复杂sql
--@testPoint 复杂表达式绑定参数(多表)+ 复杂sql+ right outer join
declare
  vl_PL2238 number;
  id_EntryDate date;
  vd_forwardPre date;
  il_AssetID number;
  vc_bigassetclass varchar2(4000);
  vl_PL2448 number;
begin
for vcur_taForward in(
  select * from 
  (select
    decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fc_fundcode) fc_fundcode, fc_tatype, fc_seller, fd_filingdate, nvl(fd_taconfirm,fd_entrydate) fd_taconfirm,
	fc_outfundcode, decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fl_fundid) fl_fundid,
	fl_batchid, nvl(fl_holderid,0) fl_holderid, ff_exchangerate, fc_currency,
	sum(ff_quantity) ff_quantity, sum(ff_amount) ff_amount, sum(ff_redeemfee) ff_redeemfee, sum(ff_confiscate) ff_confiscate,
	sum(ff_behindallotfee) ff_behindallotfee, sum(ff_performreward) ff_performreward, sum(ff_switchoutnetfree) ff_switchoutnetfree,
	sum(ff_paidincaptal) ff_paidincaptal, sum(ff_unrealizedcapital) ff_unrealizedcapital,
	sum(decode(fc_biztype,'1121',ff_amount,ff_cashamount)) ff_cashamount,
	sum(ff_unpaycashamount) ff_unpaycashamount, sum(ff_navout) ff_navout, sum(ff_etfconvert) ff_etfconvert,
	sum(ff_floatmanagerfee) ff_floatmanagerfee, sum(ff_investperformreward) ff_investperformreward, sum(ff_sellerperformreward) ff_sellerperformreawrd,
	sum(ff_performrewardriskfee) ff_performrewardriskfee,
	min(fl_id) fl_id,
	min(fd_entrydate) fd_entrydate,
	case when substr(decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fc_fundcode),1,3) = '511' and vc_bigassetclass = '0200' and fc_biztype = '3000' then 1
	    else 0 end fl_flag1116
  from (select a.*,decode(a.fc_biztype,'3021','3000','3022','3001','3019','3010','1121','3010',a.fc_biztype) fc_tatype
        from test_ydbrd_28741_plsql2_t1 a
		where a.fl_assetid = il_AssetID
		and   a.fd_entrydate <= id_EntryDate
		and   a.fd_filingdate >= vd_forwardPre
		and   a.fd_filingdate <= id_EntryDate
		and   not (vl_PL2238 = 2 and fc_biztype in ('3021','3000'))
		and   a.fl_bizmode = 1
	)
  group by decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fc_fundcode),fc_tatype,fc_seller,fd_filingdate,fd_taconfirm,fd_entrydate,
  fc_outfundcode, decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fl_fundid),
  fl_batchid, nvl(fl_holderid,0), ff_exchangerate, fc_currency,
  vc_bigassetclass,fc_biztype
  ) t1 right outer join 
  (select
    decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fc_fundcode) fc_fundcode, fc_tatype, fc_seller, fd_filingdate, nvl(fd_taconfirm,fd_entrydate) fd_taconfirm,
	fc_outfundcode, decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fl_fundid) fl_fundid,
	fl_batchid, nvl(fl_holderid,0) fl_holderid, ff_exchangerate, fc_currency,
	sum(ff_quantity) ff_quantity, sum(ff_amount) ff_amount, sum(ff_redeemfee) ff_redeemfee, sum(ff_confiscate) ff_confiscate,
	sum(ff_behindallotfee) ff_behindallotfee, sum(ff_performreward) ff_performreward, sum(ff_switchoutnetfree) ff_switchoutnetfree,
	sum(ff_paidincaptal) ff_paidincaptal, sum(ff_unrealizedcapital) ff_unrealizedcapital,
	sum(decode(fc_biztype,'1121',ff_amount,ff_cashamount)) ff_cashamount,
	sum(ff_unpaycashamount) ff_unpaycashamount, sum(ff_navout) ff_navout, sum(ff_etfconvert) ff_etfconvert,
	sum(ff_floatmanagerfee) ff_floatmanagerfee, sum(ff_investperformreward) ff_investperformreward, sum(ff_sellerperformreward) ff_sellerperformreawrd,
	sum(ff_performrewardriskfee) ff_performrewardriskfee,
	min(fl_id) fl_id,
	min(fd_entrydate) fd_entrydate,
	case when substr(decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fc_fundcode),1,3) = '511' and vc_bigassetclass = '0200' and fc_biztype = '3000' then 1
	    else 0 end fl_flag1116
  from (select a.*,decode(a.fc_biztype,'3021','3000','3022','3001','3019','3010','1121','3010',a.fc_biztype) fc_tatype
        from test_ydbrd_28741_plsql2_t1 a
		where a.fl_assetid = il_AssetID
		and   a.fd_entrydate <= id_EntryDate
		and   a.fd_filingdate >= vd_forwardPre
		and   a.fd_filingdate <= id_EntryDate
		and   not (vl_PL2238 = 2 and fc_biztype in ('3021','3000'))
		and   a.fl_bizmode = 1
	)
  group by decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fc_fundcode),fc_tatype,fc_seller,fd_filingdate,fd_taconfirm,fd_entrydate,
  fc_outfundcode, decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fl_fundid),
  fl_batchid, nvl(fl_holderid,0), ff_exchangerate, fc_currency,
  vc_bigassetclass,fc_biztype
  ) t2
  on t1.fc_fundcode = t2.fc_fundcode
  )
  loop
    dbms_output.put_line('abcd');
  end loop;
end;
/

--@testPoint 复杂表达式绑定参数(多表)+ 复杂sql+ full join
declare
  vl_PL2238 number;
  id_EntryDate date;
  vd_forwardPre date;
  il_AssetID number;
  vc_bigassetclass varchar2(4000);
  vl_PL2448 number;
begin
for vcur_taForward in(
  select * from 
  (select
    decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fc_fundcode) fc_fundcode, fc_tatype, fc_seller, fd_filingdate, nvl(fd_taconfirm,fd_entrydate) fd_taconfirm,
	fc_outfundcode, decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fl_fundid) fl_fundid,
	fl_batchid, nvl(fl_holderid,0) fl_holderid, ff_exchangerate, fc_currency,
	sum(ff_quantity) ff_quantity, sum(ff_amount) ff_amount, sum(ff_redeemfee) ff_redeemfee, sum(ff_confiscate) ff_confiscate,
	sum(ff_behindallotfee) ff_behindallotfee, sum(ff_performreward) ff_performreward, sum(ff_switchoutnetfree) ff_switchoutnetfree,
	sum(ff_paidincaptal) ff_paidincaptal, sum(ff_unrealizedcapital) ff_unrealizedcapital,
	sum(decode(fc_biztype,'1121',ff_amount,ff_cashamount)) ff_cashamount,
	sum(ff_unpaycashamount) ff_unpaycashamount, sum(ff_navout) ff_navout, sum(ff_etfconvert) ff_etfconvert,
	sum(ff_floatmanagerfee) ff_floatmanagerfee, sum(ff_investperformreward) ff_investperformreward, sum(ff_sellerperformreward) ff_sellerperformreawrd,
	sum(ff_performrewardriskfee) ff_performrewardriskfee,
	min(fl_id) fl_id,
	min(fd_entrydate) fd_entrydate,
	case when substr(decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fc_fundcode),1,3) = '511' and vc_bigassetclass = '0200' and fc_biztype = '3000' then 1
	    else 0 end fl_flag1116
  from (select a.*,decode(a.fc_biztype,'3021','3000','3022','3001','3019','3010','1121','3010',a.fc_biztype) fc_tatype
        from test_ydbrd_28741_plsql2_t1 a
		where a.fl_assetid = il_AssetID
		and   a.fd_entrydate <= id_EntryDate
		and   a.fd_filingdate >= vd_forwardPre
		and   a.fd_filingdate <= id_EntryDate
		and   not (vl_PL2238 = 2 and fc_biztype in ('3021','3000'))
		and   a.fl_bizmode = 1
	)
  group by decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fc_fundcode),fc_tatype,fc_seller,fd_filingdate,fd_taconfirm,fd_entrydate,
  fc_outfundcode, decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fl_fundid),
  fl_batchid, nvl(fl_holderid,0), ff_exchangerate, fc_currency,
  vc_bigassetclass,fc_biztype
  ) t1 full join 
  (select
    decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fc_fundcode) fc_fundcode, fc_tatype, fc_seller, fd_filingdate, nvl(fd_taconfirm,fd_entrydate) fd_taconfirm,
	fc_outfundcode, decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fl_fundid) fl_fundid,
	fl_batchid, nvl(fl_holderid,0) fl_holderid, ff_exchangerate, fc_currency,
	sum(ff_quantity) ff_quantity, sum(ff_amount) ff_amount, sum(ff_redeemfee) ff_redeemfee, sum(ff_confiscate) ff_confiscate,
	sum(ff_behindallotfee) ff_behindallotfee, sum(ff_performreward) ff_performreward, sum(ff_switchoutnetfree) ff_switchoutnetfree,
	sum(ff_paidincaptal) ff_paidincaptal, sum(ff_unrealizedcapital) ff_unrealizedcapital,
	sum(decode(fc_biztype,'1121',ff_amount,ff_cashamount)) ff_cashamount,
	sum(ff_unpaycashamount) ff_unpaycashamount, sum(ff_navout) ff_navout, sum(ff_etfconvert) ff_etfconvert,
	sum(ff_floatmanagerfee) ff_floatmanagerfee, sum(ff_investperformreward) ff_investperformreward, sum(ff_sellerperformreward) ff_sellerperformreawrd,
	sum(ff_performrewardriskfee) ff_performrewardriskfee,
	min(fl_id) fl_id,
	min(fd_entrydate) fd_entrydate,
	case when substr(decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fc_fundcode),1,3) = '511' and vc_bigassetclass = '0200' and fc_biztype = '3000' then 1
	    else 0 end fl_flag1116
  from (select a.*,decode(a.fc_biztype,'3021','3000','3022','3001','3019','3010','1121','3010',a.fc_biztype) fc_tatype
        from test_ydbrd_28741_plsql2_t1 a
		where a.fl_assetid = il_AssetID
		and   a.fd_entrydate <= id_EntryDate
		and   a.fd_filingdate >= vd_forwardPre
		and   a.fd_filingdate <= id_EntryDate
		and   not (vl_PL2238 = 2 and fc_biztype in ('3021','3000'))
		and   a.fl_bizmode = 1
	)
  group by decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fc_fundcode),fc_tatype,fc_seller,fd_filingdate,fd_taconfirm,fd_entrydate,
  fc_outfundcode, decode(vl_PL2448, 1, fl_fundid),
  fl_batchid, nvl(fl_holderid,0), ff_exchangerate, fc_currency,
  vc_bigassetclass,fc_biztype
  ) t2
  on t1.fc_fundcode = t2.fc_fundcode
  )
  loop
    dbms_output.put_line('abcd');
  end loop;
end;
/

drop table test_ydbrd_28741_plsql2_t2;

drop table if exists  tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_06_bit;
create table tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_06_bit(id int,id1 double,c0 char(20),c1 varchar(20),c2 nchar(20),c3 nvarchar(20),c4 raw(10),c5 clob,c6 blob,c7 bit(64));
insert into tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_06_bit values(null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null);
insert into tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_06_bit values(null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null);
insert into tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_06_bit values(null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null);

begin
	for i in 1 .. 10 loop
    insert into tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_06_bit values(25+i,25+i,b'011'||mod(i,2),b'011'||mod(i,2),b'011'||mod(i,2),b'011'||mod(i,2),'ABC'||i,'ABC'||i,'ABC'||i,b'011'||mod(i,2));
	end loop;
end;
/
commit;
select * from tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_06_bit;
drop table if exists  tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_05_bit;
create table tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_05_bit(id int,id1 double,c0 char(20),c1 varchar(20),c2 nchar(20),c3 nvarchar(20),c4 raw(10),c5 clob,c6 blob,c7 bit(64));
insert into tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_05_bit values(null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null);
insert into tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_05_bit values(null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null);
insert into tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_05_bit values(null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null,null);

begin
	for i in 1 .. 10 loop
    insert into tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_05_bit values(25+i,25+i,b'011'||mod(i,2),b'011'||mod(i,2),b'011'||mod(i,2),b'011'||mod(i,2),'ABC'||i,'ABC'||i,'ABC'||i,b'011'||mod(i,2));
	end loop;
end;
/
commit;
select * from tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_05_bit;
create or replace procedure proc1(
    id1 in int
) is
    res int :=0;
begin
    select count(t1.id) into res from tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_05_bit t1 inner join tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_06_bit t2 on t1.c7 = t2.id and t2.id1 = t2.id and t2.id <=id1;
    dbms_output.put_line('id1:'|| res);
end;
/
call proc1(31);
 drop table if exists  tb_YDBRD_34800_datatype_05_bit;

-- @testpoint: char类型隐式转换为varchar
insert into table_34290_15_4_2 select * from table_34290_15_4_1;
select c1,c2,c3,CHAR_LENGTH(c2),CHAR_LENGTH(c3) from table_34290_15_4_1;
select c1,c2,c3,CHAR_LENGTH(c2),CHAR_LENGTH(c3) from table_34290_15_4_2;

set session sql_mode=CONCAT(@@session.sql_mode, ',PAD_CHAR_TO_FULL_LENGTH');

select c1,c2,c3,CHAR_LENGTH(c2),CHAR_LENGTH(c3) from table_34290_15_4_2;

-- 再次insert into select后查询
insert into table_34290_15_4_2 select * from table_34290_15_4_1;
select c1,c2,c3,CHAR_LENGTH(c2),CHAR_LENGTH(c3) from table_34290_15_4_2;

-- clean
drop table if exists table_34290_15_4_1;

--description:udp调用utl
--author:xuyao
!rm -rf `pwd`/UTL_TEST_UDP_01
!mkdir `pwd`/UTL_TEST_UDP_01
set serveroutput on;
--@testPoint 创建并调用PACKAGE往一个文件中追加写入数据
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE pkg IS
  procedure p1;
  procedure p2;
  procedure p3;
END pkg;
/
  
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY pkg IS
HANDLE UTL_FILE.FILE_TYPE;
BUFFER_1 VARCHAR2(20000);
BUFFER_2 VARCHAR2(20000);
procedure p1 is
  begin
  BUFFER_1 := rpad('putline write',100,'abcdefghijklmn');
    IF UTL_FILE.is_open(HANDLE) THEN
        DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('p1 test1.txt已经被打开');
    ELSE
        HANDLE := UTL_FILE.fopen('`pwd`/UTL_TEST_UDP_01','test1.txt','a',32000);
        DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('p1 test1.txt第一次打开');
    END IF;
    UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(HANDLE,BUFFER_1);
    UTL_FILE.fflush(HANDLE);
  end;
  
procedure p2 is
  begin
  BUFFER_2 := rpad('put write',100,'123456789');
    IF UTL_FILE.is_open(HANDLE) THEN
        DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('p1 test1.txt已经被打开');
    ELSE
        HANDLE := UTL_FILE.fopen('`pwd`/UTL_TEST_UDP_01','test1.txt','a',32000);
        DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('p1 OPEN FILE');
    END IF;
    for i in 1 .. 10 loop
    UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(HANDLE,BUFFER_2);
    UTL_FILE.fflush(HANDLE);
	end loop;
  end;
 
procedure p3 is
  begin
     UTL_FILE.fclose(HANDLE);
  end;
  
END pkg;
/
--调用p1
call pkg.p1()
/
!cat `pwd`/UTL_TEST_UDP_01/test1.txt
--调用p2
call pkg.p2()
/
!cat `pwd`/UTL_TEST_UDP_01/test1.txt
--调用p3
call pkg.p3()
/

--@testPoint 创建并调用PACKAGE,从一个数据文件读出数据并写入到另外一个文件中
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE rw_pkg IS
  procedure p1;
  procedure p2;
  procedure p3;
END rw_pkg;
/
CREATE OR REPLACE PACKAGE BODY rw_pkg IS
HANDLE_W UTL_FILE.FILE_TYPE;
HANDLE_R UTL_FILE.FILE_TYPE;
BUFFER VARCHAR2(20000);
procedure p1 is
begin
HANDLE_R := UTL_FILE.fopen('`pwd`/UTL_TEST_UDP_01','test1.txt','R',32000);
HANDLE_W := UTL_FILE.fopen('`pwd`/UTL_TEST_UDP_01','test2.txt','W',32000);	
for i in 1..10 loop
UTL_FILE.fseek(HANDLE_R,2,3);
UTL_FILE.GET_LINE(HANDLE_R,BUFFER,10);
UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(HANDLE_W,BUFFER,false);
UTL_FILE.fflush(HANDLE_W);
END LOOP;
END;

procedure p2 is
begin
HANDLE_R := UTL_FILE.fopen('`pwd`/UTL_TEST_UDP_01','test1.txt','R',32000);
HANDLE_W := UTL_FILE.fopen('`pwd`/UTL_TEST_UDP_01','test3.txt','W',32000);	
for i in 1..10 loop
UTL_FILE.GET_LINE(HANDLE_R,BUFFER,10);
UTL_FILE.PUT_LINE(HANDLE_W,BUFFER,false);
UTL_FILE.fflush(HANDLE_W);
END LOOP;
END;

procedure p3 is
  begin
     UTL_FILE.fclose_all();
  end;
  
END rw_pkg;
/
--调用p1
call rw_pkg.p1()
/
!cat `pwd`/UTL_TEST_UDP_01/test2.txt
--调用p2
call rw_pkg.p2()
/
!cat `pwd`/UTL_TEST_UDP_01/test3.txt
--调用p3
call rw_pkg.p3()
/
!rm -rf `pwd`/UTL_TEST_UDP_01/test2.txt
!rm -rf `pwd`/UTL_TEST_UDP_01/test3.txt

--@testPoint 创建一个udt名叫HANDLE_W，在utl_file中使用这个udt
call rw_pkg.p3()
/
create or replace type HANDLE_W as object(a int);
/
DECLARE
HANDLE_W UTL_FILE.FILE_TYPE;
BUFFER_W VARCHAR2(8000);
BEGIN
if UTL_FILE.is_open(HANDLE_W) THEN
DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('test1.txt已经被打开');
else
HANDLE_W := UTL_FILE.fopen('`pwd`/UTL_TEST_UDP_01','test1.txt','w',100);
DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('test1.txt第一次打开'); 
end if;
UTL_FILE.FCLOSE(HANDLE_W);
end;
/

--@testPoint select查询写到文件中去
--创建表
create table test_sdv_ydbrd_13358_table_01 (    
    c1 tinyint not null,
    c2 smallint,
    c3 int default 1e2,
    c4 bigint,
    c5 float check(length(c5) >0),
    c6 double,
    c7 number,
    c8 char(100),
    c9 varchar(4000),
    c10 date,
    c11 time,
    c12 timestamp,
    c13 INTERVAL YEAR(4) TO MONTH,
    c14 INTERVAL DAY(9) TO SECOND(9),
    c15 boolean,
    c16 raw(16),
    c17 clob
    ) organization heap;

end;

CREATE or replace TYPE super_obj_type_method_007_2 force AS OBJECT 
   ( year NUMBER, 
     member function mem_f1(y_in number)return varchar ,
     member procedure mem_proc1(y_in number) 
   )NOT FINAL; 
/

CREATE or replace TYPE body super_obj_type_method_007_2 AS
member function mem_f1(y_in number)return varchar as
begin
  return self.year;
end;
member procedure mem_proc1(y_in number) as
BEGIN
  self.year := self.year+y_in;
  dbms_output.put_line('mem_proc1: year: '||self.year);
END;
end;
/

create or replace type sub_obj_type_method_007_2 under super_obj_type_method_007_2
(month number
)not final;
/



drop type if exists varray_type_method_007_2;
create type varray_type_method_007_2 as varray(200) of sub_obj_type_method_007_2;
/

CREATE OR REPLACE function udf_type_method_007_2(p1 sub_obj_type_method_007_2) return varray_type_method_007_2 AS
BEGIN
  return varray_type_method_007_2(p1,p1,null);
END;
/

BEGIN
  dbms_output.put_line(udf_type_method_007_2(sub_obj_type_method_007_2(2021,10))(1).year);
END;
/

BEGIN
  dbms_output.put_line(udf_type_method_007_2(sub_obj_type_method_007_2(2021,10))(1).mem_f1(null));
END;
/

BEGIN
  udf_type_method_007_2(sub_obj_type_method_007_2(2021,10))(1).mem_proc1(9);
END;
/

obj1 sub_obj_type_method_007_2 := sub_obj_type_method_007_2(2001,1);

BEGIN

END;
/


drop type varray_type_method_007_2;
drop type  sub_obj_type_method_007_2;

--author: dangwenqi
--test_sdv_ydbrd26092_028
set serveroutput on;
set define off;
--pkg1中定义的record，做过程体入参，pkg1.var，dml，用于delete/update
drop table if exists tb_ydbrd26092_028;
drop package if exists pkg_ydbrd26092_028;
drop procedure if exists proc_ydbrd26092_028;

create table tb_ydbrd26092_028(c1 number,c2 varchar(3000));

begin
    for i in 1 .. 10 loop
        insert into tb_ydbrd26092_028 values(i,lpad('test1',i*4,'测试语句是这样'));
    end loop;
    commit;
end;
/

create or replace package pkg_ydbrd26092_028 as
    type type_ydbrd26092_028 is varray(300) of varchar(2000);
    type type_001 is record(c1 int, c2 type_ydbrd26092_028);
end;
/

--报错，对象必须是sql type
create or replace procedure proc_ydbrd26092_028(c1 in int,x1 in pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_001 := pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_001(6,pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_ydbrd26092_028(6,'dddddddddddd'))) as
begin
    update tb_ydbrd26092_028 set c2 = x1.c2(x1.c2.last) where c1 = x1.c1+1;
    delete from tb_ydbrd26092_028 where lengthb(c2) < lengthb(x1.c2(x1.c2.last));
    commit;
end;
/

create or replace procedure proc_ydbrd26092_028(c1 in int,x1 in pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_001 := pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_001(6,pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_ydbrd26092_028(6,'dddddddddddd'))) as
begin
    update tb_ydbrd26092_028 set c2 = 'test' where c1 = x1.c1+1;
    --delete from tb_ydbrd26092_028 where lengthb(c2) < lengthb(x1.c2(x1.c2.last));
    commit;
end;
/

create or replace procedure proc_ydbrd26092_028(c1 in int,x1 in pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_001 := pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_001(6,pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_ydbrd26092_028(6,'dddddddddddd'))) as
begin
    update tb_ydbrd26092_028 set c2 = x1.c2(x1.c2.last) where c1 = 1;
    --delete from tb_ydbrd26092_028 where lengthb(c2) < lengthb(x1.c2(x1.c2.last));
    commit;
end;
/


select c1,lengthb(c2) from tb_ydbrd26092_028 order by 1;

declare
    rec1 pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_001 := pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_001(5, pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_ydbrd26092_028(2,lpad('test2',100,'测试用的语句包含，##的🍔🍟🥤')));
    a int := 4;
begin
    proc_ydbrd26092_028(a,rec1);
    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('out:  '||a);
    for i in rec1.c2.first .. rec1.c2.last loop
        DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('rec1.after.c2('||i||'):  '||rec1.c2(i));
    end loop;
end;
/
--成功
create or replace procedure proc_ydbrd26092_028(b1 in int,x1 in pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_001 := pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_001(6,pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_ydbrd26092_028(6,'dddddddddddd'))) as
    a1 number;
begin
    update tb_ydbrd26092_028 t set t.c2 = lpad('c2c2c',x1.c1,'here') where t.c1 = b1;
    a1 := lengthb(x1.c2(x1.c2.last))/10;
    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('a1:  '||a1);
    delete from tb_ydbrd26092_028 where lengthb(c2) < a1;
    commit;
end;
/

declare
    rec1 pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_001 := pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_001(5, pkg_ydbrd26092_028.type_ydbrd26092_028(2,lpad('test2',100,'测试用的语句包含，##的🍔🍟🥤')));
    a int := 4;
begin
    proc_ydbrd26092_028(a,rec1);
    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('out:  '||a);
    for i in rec1.c2.first .. rec1.c2.last loop
        DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('rec1.after.c2('||i||'):  '||rec1.c2(i));
    end loop;
end;
/

select * from tb_ydbrd26092_028 order by 1;

drop table tb_ydbrd26092_028;
drop package pkg_ydbrd26092_028;
drop procedure proc_ydbrd26092_028;
